{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783082102-bn6f9cLSAj77WBMCIaYS","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"Write_8","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/process_hwpx.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nProcess HWPX template with JSON data, applying special transformations.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport os\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\nfrom xml.dom import minidom\n\n# Register namespace to avoid prefix mangling\nET.register_namespace('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n\n# Namespace mapping\nNS = {'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'}\nHP_NS = '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}'\n\ndef load_json_data(json_path):\n    \"\"\"Load JSON data from file.\"\"\"\n    with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef transform_attendee_count(value):\n    \"\"\"Convert to digits only. E.g., '25명' -> '25'.\"\"\"\n    digits = re.sub(r'[^0-9]', '', str(value))\n    return digits\n\ndef transform_satisfaction(value):\n    \"\"\"Rewrite as X.X점 (5.0점 만점) style.\"\"\"\n    # Handle formats like \"4.5/5.0\" or \"4.5\" or \"4.5점\"\n    value_str = str(value)\n    # Extract the numeric part before any slash or Korean character\n    match = re.match(r'(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)', value_str)\n    if match:\n        score = match.group(1)\n        return f\"{score}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n    return value_str\n\ndef transform_overall_opinion(value):\n    \"\"\"Append 후속 심화반 검토 요망. to the opinion.\"\"\"\n    return f\"{value} 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n\ndef get_transformed_value(key, value):\n    \"\"\"Apply transformations based on key name.\"\"\"\n    if key == '참석자수':\n        return transform_attendee_count(value)\n    elif key == '만족도':\n        return transform_satisfaction(value)\n    elif key == '종합의견':\n        return transform_overall_opinion(value)\n    else:\n        return value\n\ndef find_placeholders(text):\n    \"\"\"Find all {{placeholder}} patterns in text.\"\"\"\n    return re.findall(r'\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}', text)\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text, data):\n    \"\"\"Replace all placeholders in text with transformed values.\"\"\"\n    placeholders = find_placeholders(text)\n    modified = False\n    for placeholder in placeholders:\n        if placeholder in data:\n            transformed = get_transformed_value(placeholder, data[placeholder])\n            text = text.replace(f'{{{{{placeholder}}}}}', transformed)\n            modified = True\n    return text, modified\n\ndef remove_linesegarray_from_para(para_elem):\n    \"\"\"Remove linesegarray child elements from a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    # Find and remove linesegarray elements (case-insensitive for local name)\n    for child in list(para_elem):\n        # Get tag name without namespace\n        tag = child.tag\n        if tag.startswith(HP_NS):\n            local_name = tag[len(HP_NS):]\n            if local_name.lower() == 'linesegarray':\n                para_elem.remove(child)\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path, data):\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file, replacing placeholders and removing stale layout cache.\"\"\"\n    # Parse XML while preserving namespace prefixes\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # Track if any modifications were made\n    any_modified = False\n\n    # Find all text elements (hp:t) anywhere in the tree\n    for text_elem in root.iter(f'{HP_NS}t'):\n        if text_elem.text:\n            original_text = text_elem.text\n            new_text, modified = replace_placeholders(original_text, data)\n            if modified:\n                text_elem.text = new_text\n                any_modified = True\n\n                # Find parent paragraph and remove linesegarray\n                # Walk up the tree to find the containing hp:p element\n                parent_map = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in p}\n                current = text_elem\n                while current is not None:\n                    parent = parent_map.get(current)\n                    if parent is not None and parent.tag == f'{HP_NS}p':\n                        remove_linesegarray_from_para(parent)\n                        break\n                    current = parent\n\n    # Write back the modified XML with proper formatting\n    # Use the original XML declaration format\n    xml_declaration = '<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\"?>\\n'\n\n    # Convert to string\n    rough_string = ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8')\n\n    # Pretty print (optional, but keeps it clean)\n    reparsed = minidom.parseString(rough_string)\n    pretty = reparsed.toprettyxml(indent=\"  \", encoding='utf-8')\n\n    # Remove extra whitespace lines that minidom adds\n    lines = pretty.decode('utf-8').split('\\n')\n    clean_lines = [line for line in lines if line.strip()]\n    clean_xml = '\\n'.join(clean_lines)\n\n    # Ensure proper XML declaration\n    if clean_xml.startswith('<?xml'):\n        # Replace the declaration with our desired format\n        clean_xml = re.sub(r'<\\?xml[^?]*\\?>', xml_declaration.strip(), clean_xml, count=1)\n    else:\n        clean_xml = xml_declaration.strip() + '\\n' + clean_xml\n\n    with open(xml_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(clean_xml)\n\n    return any_modified\n\ndef verify_no_placeholders(xml_path):\n    \"\"\"Check that no {{...}} placeholders remain in the file.\"\"\"\n    with open(xml_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    remaining = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', content)\n    return len(remaining) == 0, remaining\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    json_path = '/root/training_feedback.json'\n    extracted_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work/template_extracted'\n    xml_files = [\n        os.path.join(extracted_dir, 'Contents', 'section0.xml'),\n        os.path.join(extracted_dir, 'Contents', 'section1.xml'),\n    ]\n\n    # Load JSON data\n    data = load_json_data(json_path)\n    print(\"Loaded JSON data:\")\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n    print()\n\n    # Process each XML file\n    for xml_path in xml_files:\n        print(f\"Processing {xml_path}...\")\n        modified = process_xml_file(xml_path, data)\n        print(f\"  Modified: {modified}\")\n\n        # Verify no placeholders remain\n        clean, remaining = verify_no_placeholders(xml_path)\n        if clean:\n            print(\"  ✓ No placeholders remain\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  ✗ Placeholders still present: {remaining}\")\n            return False\n        print()\n\n    return True\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    success = main()\n    exit(0 if success else 1)\n"}}],"model":"moonshotai/kimi-k2.5-0127","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"Moonshot AI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ca306f19-7b7c-4d61-a4ee-f1c74faa64da","uuid":"6c02ffd4-7881-4fba-81ee-c3422cf6fc02"}
